CONTEXT: negative-result measurement paper. Since the last round the authors made ~25 edits in
response to a review. This round verifies that the edits are correct and complete, and looks for
NEW instances of the same error classes.

KNOWN IN-FLIGHT (do NOT report these as problems; they are being handled):
  - the realhr sweep (cap6 landed; cap8/cap4_r2/foyer_r2 still running) and the re-runs of four
    runs flagged for host co-tenancy (bres_r2 running; hz0_r2/hz2_r2/breshz1_r2/cap1_r2 queued).
    Section VI and Table I are expected to be updated when those land.
  - the Table I dagger marks and the "being re-measured" wording.

SCOPE -- five checks, be mechanical, cite file:line, report MATCH/MISMATCH with recomputed values:

(1) THE HOST-TIER CLAIM. paper/data/reuse.csv now has 24 rows (all 200-session HiCache runs) and
the paper claims realization holds at "72.8--92.5% across the nineteen host-tier runs" (see
sec_model.tex, sec_intro.tex, sec_discussion.tex). Verify: (a) the count 19 and the endpoints from
reuse.csv and from the raw logs results/night/pois200_*/steps.jsonl; (b) that no OTHER host-tier run
(any pois200_* with hicache_enabled in metadata.json) realizes below 72.8% or above 92.5%; (c) that
the excluded run foyer_hz1_r2 really does have an engine prompt-token counter ~1.41x the trace plan
(see its metrics.csv sglang:prompt_tokens_total vs the trace's planned total) with
sglang:num_retracted_reqs == 0 and no duplicate/failed steps in steps.jsonl.

(2) TABLE I's CONCURRENCY COLUMN. The no-control row now reads 94.9 with a caption footnote saying
that arm's Conc. comes from session spans because it writes no admission log, and that on the four
static caps that estimator agrees with the admission-log one. Recompute both estimators for cap1,
cap2, cap5 and cap2_25 and check the claim "agrees to two decimals"; recompute 94.9 for
pois200_default.

(3) THE SF=0 PROBE. sec_results.tex now discloses that the two aggregate indicators the probe was
pre-registered on barely moved (admitting cycles 2.35% -> 2.44%, blocked cycles 93.3% -> 93.2%)
while the composition within blocked cycles moved (remainder 54.8% -> 39.7%, over-budget 38.4% ->
44.0%). Recompute all four pairs from results/night/pois200_foyer_{hc,hc_r2,sf0_r2}/controller.jsonl
(blocked = waiting_n>0 and n_admitted==0; over-budget uses max(resident,ctx_sum)+growth_sum > budget,
excluding valve and gate_full cycles). Check the paper's numbers and the claim that the repeat
(hc_r2) reads 58.3% remainder.

(4) NEW NUMBERS THAT MUST BE IN A DATA FILE. Verify each of these appears in paper/data/*.csv:
tier25.csv (Table II, now generated by gen_tier25.py), projection.csv's growth_p50 = 26762 for
pois200_foyer_hc, gate_attrib.csv's forced_admits=51 / total_admits=206 / cycles_over_budget=3700 /
admitted_while_over=9, toolwait.csv's ratio_med=1.0007 and ratio_ratio_of_means=1.0652,
arrival_slo.csv's move_pp column (foyfix_hc 8.22, aimd2 3.22, and none other above 2.5 in absolute
value). Report any number in the paper that still has no data file.

(5) SAME NUMBER, DIFFERENT MEANING. Enumerate every numeral that appears with two or more distinct
meanings and flag any place the text does not disambiguate. Known pairs to check specifically:
36.3 (cap1's idle share) vs 36.4 (device-only realization floor); 92.4/92.5; 0.55; 1.79 (projection
ratio vs anything else); 80.6 (foyer_hc round-1+ hit rate) vs 80.2 (bres).

OUTPUT: ## (1) ... ## (5) ... then ## Acceptance assessment with a rough probability range and what
it rests on. A judgement, not a prediction. Do not invent scores.

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""前缀复用：trace 定的上界 vs 配置兑现了多少 —— paper/data/reuse.csv。

出发点是一个此前没被拆开的量：**总复用** = 设备侧命中 + host 侧接住。

上界必须【独立于观测】定义，否则是循环论证。曾经写成「数据里最多复用的量就是
天花板」，审稿人 2026-09-24 直接指出这是拿观测定义上界。真正的上界来自 trace 本身：
把 `data/replay_pois200_l04.csv` 每一行的 prefix_len 加起来 —— 那是这条 trace 的
前缀结构允许被复用的 token 总量，任何策略都不可能超过它。实测值只用来比较，
不用来定义。

本脚本输出的 realized_frac = 实测复用 / trace 上界。这一个数就是「兑现了多少」。

用法: python3 gen_reuse.py <trace.csv> <eviction.csv> <arm_running.csv> <decomp.csv> <out.csv> [concurrency.csv]

上游 eviction.csv 必须带 prompt_tok/reuse_tok/uncached_tok 三列（未舍入原始计数）——
比例一律用它们算，不用舍入到 0.1M 的列。曾经用舍入列算，cap1 印成 69.8% 而真值 69.9%。
"""
import csv, os, sys


def load(p):
    return {r["run"]: r for r in csv.DictReader(l for l in open(p) if not l.startswith("#"))}


def trace_ceiling(path):
    """trace 允许的复用上界：所有步 prefix_len 之和，以及 prompt 总量。"""
    rows = list(csv.DictReader(open(path)))
    reuse = sum(int(r["prefix_len"]) for r in rows)
    prompt = sum(int(r["prefix_len"]) + int(r["input_len"]) for r in rows)
    r0 = sum(int(r["prefix_len"]) for r in rows if int(r["round_idx"]) >= 1)
    return {"steps": len(rows),
            "sessions": len({r["session_id"] for r in rows}),
            "ceiling_tok": reuse, "prompt_tok": prompt,
            "ceiling_frac": reuse / prompt,
            "ceiling_r1_tok": r0}


def main():
    tr_p, ev_p, ar_p, dc_p, out_p = sys.argv[1:6]
    ce = trace_ceiling(tr_p)
    ev, ar, dc = load(ev_p), load(ar_p), load(dc_p)

    # 争用标记：被主机争用抬高了 tau 的 run，其 tau 列不可用。
    # 复用/重算是【体积】，与时间无关，实测这四条臂与同族干净 run 一致（见下）。
    conc = {}
    if len(sys.argv) > 6 and os.path.exists(sys.argv[6]):
        # 去 pois200_ 前缀再配对：eviction.csv 用短名，concurrency.csv 用 run 目录名。
        # 不归一化会静默全部落空（2026-09-24 犯过一次）。
        conc = {r["run"].replace("pois200_", ""): r["verdict"] for r in
                csv.DictReader(l for l in open(sys.argv[6]) if not l.startswith("#"))}
        assert len(conc) > 50, "concurrency.csv 只读到 %d 行" % len(conc)

    rows, bad = [], []
    for run, e in ev.items():
        a, d = ar.get(run), dc.get(run)
        if not a or not d:
            bad.append((run, "arm_running 或 decomp 里没有这条"))
            continue
        if "prompt_tok" not in e:
            bad.append((run, "eviction.csv 缺 prompt_tok —— 请用新版 gen_eviction.py 重跑"))
            continue

        prompt = int(e["prompt_tok"])
        reuse = int(e["reuse_tok"])
        unc = int(e["uncached_tok"])
        # 用精确列，不用 0.1M 取整列 —— 同表的教训（算比例必须走未取整值）
        dev_miss = int(e["dev_miss_tok"])
        host_hit = int(e["host_hit_tok"])

        # 断言 1：设备未命中 = host 接住 + 真正重算（这套记账的定义式；
        # 挡住的是从 eviction.csv 抄错列——抄错不会有这个性质）
        assert dev_miss == host_hit + unc, \
            "%s: dev_miss %d != host_hit %d + uncached %d（精确列必须严格相等）" % (
                run, dev_miss, host_hit, unc)
        # 断言 2：复用 + 重算 = 总 prompt
        assert (reuse + unc) == prompt, \
            "%s: reuse %d + uncached %d != prompt %d" % (run, reuse, unc, prompt)
        # 断言 3：实测复用不得超过 trace 上界 —— 这是本表的核心不等式，
        # 若它被违反，说明口径搞错了（而不是发现了超光速）
        assert reuse <= ce["ceiling_tok"], \
            "%s: 实测复用 %d 超过 trace 上界 %d" % (run, reuse, ce["ceiling_tok"])

        rows.append({
            "run": run,
            "family": "hicache" if e["hicache"] == "1" else "device_only",
            "Rbar_busy": "%.6f" % float(a["running_busy"]),
            "prompt_tok": prompt,
            "reuse_tok": reuse,
            "reuse_host_tok": host_hit,
            "recompute_tok": unc,
            "reuse_frac": "%.4f" % (reuse / prompt),                 # 占总 prompt
            # bound_frac 必须作为【数据列】出现，不能只写在注释里 —— 审计脚本
            # 会跳过 # 开头的行，于是正文里的 75.5% 在数据文件里找不到出处
            # （2026-09-24 审计报了 9 处假阳性才发现）。与 reuse_x.csv 的列名一致。
            "bound_frac": "%.4f" % ce["ceiling_frac"],
            # 上界的【原始 token 数】也要落成列：正文引用了 21,540,371 与 28,526,871，
            # 而它们原来只在注释里（审计跳过注释行）。
            "bound_tok": ce["ceiling_tok"],
            "prompt_total_tok": ce["prompt_tok"],
            "realized_frac": "%.6f" % (reuse / ce["ceiling_tok"]),   # 占 trace 上界 <<< 关键列
            "tau1_ms": d["step_back_ms"],
            "contested": conc.get(run, "?"),
        })

    rows.sort(key=lambda r: (r["family"], float(r["Rbar_busy"])))
    header = "\n".join([
        "# 前缀复用：trace 定的上界 vs 配置兑现了多少 —— 由 scripts/lab/gen_reuse.py 生成，勿手改",
        "#",
        "# trace 上界（独立于观测，从 %s 求和）:" % os.path.basename(tr_p),
        "#   %d 会话 %d 步；sum(prefix_len) = %d tokens；prompt 总量 = %d tokens" % (
            ce["sessions"], ce["steps"], ce["ceiling_tok"], ce["prompt_tok"]),
        "#   => 计划复用率 ceiling_frac = %.4f（这就是论文里那个 75%% 的来源）" % ce["ceiling_frac"],
        "#   上界【必须】这样定义。曾经写成「数据里最多复用的量就是天花板」，是循环论证。",
        "#",
        "# realized_frac = reuse_tok / trace 上界 —— 这一个数才是「兑现了多少」。",
        "#   device-only 随 R̄ 一路掉；host tier 在控制器运行区间内守住。",
        "#   注意【没有任何配置兑现到 1.0】：实测最好也只有 %.4f。" % (19935879 / ce["ceiling_tok"]),
        "#",
        "# 比例一律由未舍入的 *_tok 列算出。曾经拿舍入到 0.1M 的列算，cap1 印成 69.8%%（真值 69.9%%）。",
        "# contested: 该 run 是否落在主机争用窗口（见 concurrency.csv）。三档含义：",
        "#   clean     = 生命周期内同机 <3 条，未受影响",
        "#   watch     = 同机 >=3 条持续 >=60 分钟，但实测 tau2 未见整体抬升（cap1 即此档）",
        "#   CONTESTED = 同机 >=4 条持续 >=60 分钟，tau1_ms 这一列不可用；",
        "#               reuse/recompute 是体积量，实测与同族干净 run 一致。",
        "run,family,Rbar_busy,prompt_tok,reuse_tok,reuse_host_tok,recompute_tok,"
        "reuse_frac,bound_frac,bound_tok,prompt_total_tok,realized_frac,tau1_ms,contested"])
    fields = ["run", "family", "Rbar_busy", "prompt_tok", "reuse_tok", "reuse_host_tok",
              "recompute_tok", "reuse_frac", "bound_frac", "bound_tok",
              "prompt_total_tok", "realized_frac", "tau1_ms", "contested"]
    body = header + "\n" + "\n".join(",".join(str(r[k]) for k in fields) for r in rows) + "\n"
    assert isinstance(body, str)
    with open(out_p, "w") as fh:
        fh.write(body)

    print("trace 上界 %d / prompt %d = %.4f" % (ce["ceiling_tok"], ce["prompt_tok"], ce["ceiling_frac"]))
    print("写入 %s：%d 行" % (out_p, len(rows)))
    for r in rows:
        print("  %-16s %-11s R̄ %-6s 复用 %10s (%.4f) 兑现 %.4f  重算 %10s  τ₁ %-6s %s" % (
            r["run"], r["family"], r["Rbar_busy"], r["reuse_tok"], float(r["reuse_frac"]),
            float(r["realized_frac"]), r["recompute_tok"], r["tau1_ms"],
            "<<CONTESTED" if r["contested"] == "CONTESTED" else ""))
    if bad:
        print("!! 未写入：")
        for n, why in bad:
            print("   %-16s %s" % (n, why))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""「有会话在等、却一个都不放行」的周期里，到底是什么在挡？

## 撤回记录（2026-09-24）

本脚本的第一版**是错的，且错得方向相反**。它用日志里的 `base` 字段算投影：

    (d.get("base") or 0) + (d.get("growth_sum") or 0) > d["budget"]

但 `base` 被 controller_budget3.py 的候选循环重绑（`base = s["ctx"] or s["prompt0"]`
写在循环内、日志写在循环后），实测在 `pois200_foyer_hc` 的 8784 个周期里
**恒为 0**。于是那一项退化成 `growth_sum` 单独一个量，报出来的
「投影超预算 0.2%」「98.3% 的周期投影 < 90% 预算」「中位 29%」
**全是 growth reserve 的统计量，不是投影的**。

按正确投影（同 gen_projection.py：`max(resident, ctx_sum) + growth_sum`）重算：
被挡周期里 **44.4% 是投影超预算**、只有 43.7% 低于 90% 预算。
也就是说拦人的主要就是**估算值**，与第一版结论相反。

## 归因的可判定性（诚实标注）

准入判定是一个合取式：

    if waiting and not valve and not gate_full and (prev_started or sf_disabled):
        逐个候选试 projection + n <= budget

- `valve`、`gate_full`、正确的 `projection` 都能从日志直接算 —— 可判定。
- `prev_started`（单飞闸门）**日志里没有**。所以「剩下的是什么」无法直接判定：
  在 sf 开着的臂上，剩下的既可能是「单飞闸门没满足」，也可能是
  「试了队首但塞不进」。本脚本把它单列一档 remainder，并明确标注为不可判定。
- **`sf=0` 的臂是天然的探针**：单飞关掉时 `(prev_started or True)` 恒真，
  闸门不可能拦人，所以它的 remainder 只可能是「没有候选者塞得进预算」。
  拿它可给出单飞贡献的上界。
"""
import glob, json, os, sys


def load(p):
    out = []
    for line in open(p):
        line = line.strip()
        if not line:
            continue
        try:
            d = json.loads(line)
        except ValueError:
            continue
        if "budget" in d and "ctx_sum" in d and "growth_sum" in d and "waiting_n" in d:
            out.append(d)
    return out


def proj(d):
    """与 gen_projection.py / 部署代码 322-326 行同一算法。"""
    return max(d.get("resident") or 0, d.get("ctx_sum") or 0) + (d.get("growth_sum") or 0)


# 论文正文引用的统计集合 —— 【显式列名】，不再用"十九条"这种无法复现的口径。
# 这是 Table I 的控制器段（九条 Foyer 变体 + 两个 AIMD 设置）。
# 九条 Foyer 变体里，有八条留了 controller.jsonl；foyfix_hc 与两个 AIMD 没有
# （AIMD 走另一套控制器），所以它们不在本表里 —— 正文必须按这八条写。
# 论文里引用的臂，显式列出（避免"十八条"这种不可复现的口径）。
# ⚠ foyer_sf0_r2 必须在里面：§V 用它的 remainder/projection-over 两个数论证
# 单飞闸门占约十五个点，而那两个数曾经【只存在于正文里、不在任何 CSV 里】，
# 且与仓库脚本复算的值对不上（正文 39.9/43.5，复算 39.7/44.0）——
# 2026-09-24 审稿人抓到的。纳进来之后正文只能引用 CSV。
PAPER_SET = ["pois200_foyer_hz0", "pois200_foyer_hz1", "pois200_foyer_hz2",
             "pois200_foyer_hc", "pois200_foyer_bres", "pois200_foyer_breshz1",
             "pois200_foyer_nogate", "pois200_foyer_rg4",
             "pois200_foyer_sf0_r2", "pois200_foyer_hc_r2"]


# 必须在实验机上跑：本表统计【所有】带 controller.jsonl 的 run，本地镜像是部分快照
# （2026-09-24 实测：本地 34 行 vs 实验机 46 行），在本地跑会把正文引用的
# 「over every run in that file」那两个中位数算错（27.0/5.4 是 22 行的旧快照，
# 实验机 46 行给 27.4/5.3）。见 CLAUDE.md「本地镜像是部分快照」。
MIN_ROWS = 40


def main():
    root = sys.argv[1]
    out_path = sys.argv[2]
    # 只统计控制器臂（有 ctx_sum 的），并把集合显式列出来，避免"十八"这种不可复现的口径
    rows = []
    for p in sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(root, "*", "controller.jsonl"))):
        run = os.path.basename(os.path.dirname(p))
        if run.startswith("_"):
            continue
        L = load(p)
        if len(L) < 200:
            continue
        # 单飞是否关闭（从 metadata 的 policy 读）
        mp = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(p), "metadata.json")
        pol = ""
        if os.path.exists(mp):
            try:
                pol = json.load(open(mp)).get("policy", "") or ""
            except ValueError:
                pass
        sf_off = "sf=0" in pol
        b = L[-1]["budget"]
        blk = [d for d in L if (d.get("waiting_n") or 0) > 0 and (d.get("n_admitted") or 0) == 0]
        if not blk:
            continue
        n = len(blk)
        valve = sum(1 for d in blk if d.get("valve"))
        gate = sum(1 for d in blk if not d.get("valve") and d.get("gate_full"))
        over = sum(1 for d in blk if not d.get("valve") and not d.get("gate_full")
                   and proj(d) > b)
        rem = n - valve - gate - over
        # starve guard：唯一绕过预算的通道。§V 用 foyer_hc 的三个数论证
        # 「投影超预算时只有它能进来」：全部超预算周期数、其中放了人的、其中被强制的。
        # 这三个数 2026-09-24 之前只在正文里（3,700 / 9 / 9），文件里没有，于是落列。
        forced = sum(1 for d in L if d.get("forced"))
        admits = sum(1 for d in L if (d.get("n_admitted") or 0) > 0)
        over_all = [d for d in L if proj(d) > b]
        over_adm = [d for d in over_all if (d.get("n_admitted") or 0) > 0]
        over_forced = [d for d in over_adm if d.get("forced")]
        rows.append(dict(run=run, n=n, blocked=n, valve=valve, gate=gate, over=over, rem=rem,
                         sf_off=sf_off, forced=forced, admits=admits, samples=len(L),
                         over_all=len(over_all), over_all_admitted=len(over_adm),
                         over_all_forced=len(over_forced)))
    assert len(rows) >= MIN_ROWS, (
        "只统计到 %d 行的闸门归因（需要 >=%d）—— 这多半是部分镜像，本脚本必须在实验机上跑"
        % (len(rows), MIN_ROWS))
    rows.sort(key=lambda r: -r["over"] / r["blocked"])
    print("%-26s %6s %8s %7s %7s %8s %9s %6s" % (
        "run", "采样", "被挡", "阀门%", "gate%", "投影超%", "remainder%", "sf关"))
    for r in rows:
        k = r["blocked"]
        print("%-26s %6d %8d %6.1f %6.1f %7.1f %9.1f %6s" % (
            r["run"], r["samples"], k, 100*r["valve"]/k, 100*r["gate"]/k,
            100*r["over"]/k, 100*r["rem"]/k, "Y" if r["sf_off"] else ""))
    with open(out_path, "w") as f:
        f.write("# 「有等待不放行」周期的闸门归因 —— gen_gate_attrib.py 生成\n")
        f.write("# 投影 = max(resident, ctx_sum) + growth_sum（【不是】被重绑污染的 base 字段）\n")
        f.write("# remainder = 排除 valve/gate/投影超预算后剩下的，含【单飞闸门】与【队首塞不进】两类，\n")
        f.write("#   日志里没有 prev_started，无法区分，故不做单飞归因。sf_off=Y 的臂是探针：\n")
        f.write("#   单飞关闭时闸门不可能拦人，其 remainder 只可能是「没有候选者塞得进预算」。\n")
        f.write("run,samples,blocked_cycles,valve_pct,round_gate_pct,projection_over_pct,"
                "remainder_pct,sf_disabled,forced_admits,total_admits,"
                "cycles_over_budget,admitted_while_over,forced_while_over\n")
        for r in rows:
            k = r["blocked"]
            f.write("%s,%d,%d,%.1f,%.1f,%.1f,%.1f,%s,%d,%d,%d,%d,%d\n" % (
                r["run"], r["samples"], k, 100*r["valve"]/k, 100*r["gate"]/k,
                100*r["over"]/k, 100*r["rem"]/k, "Y" if r["sf_off"] else "N",
                r["forced"], r["admits"], r["over_all"], r["over_all_admitted"],
                r["over_all_forced"]))
    # 论文正文用的集合，单独汇总并写进 CSV（含名单），使那两个中位数可复现
    sel = [r for r in rows if r["run"] in PAPER_SET]
    if sel:
        k = lambda key: sorted(100.0 * r[key] / r["blocked"] for r in sel)
        vp, op, rp = k("valve"), k("over"), k("rem")
        mid = lambda v: v[len(v) // 2]
        print()
        print("论文集合（%d 条，名单见 CSV 注释）：阀门中位 %.1f%%、投影超预算中位 %.1f%%、"
              "remainder 中位 %.1f%%" % (len(sel), mid(vp), mid(op), mid(rp)))
        # 正文用的集合是【八条非重复的 Foyer 臂】（foyfix 与两条 AIMD 跑的是别的
        # 控制器、不记闸门状态）。原来只落 10 条的 PAPER_SET，于是正文引的
        # 「八条」中位数在文件里查不到，而 PAPER_SET 的中位数又对不上正文
        # （2026-09-24 审稿人抓：正文把投影中位 32.7 写进了阀门那一格）。
        eight = {"foyer_bres", "foyer_hc", "foyer_hz2", "foyer_hz1",
                 "foyer_breshz1", "foyer_nogate", "foyer_hz0", "foyer_rg4"}
        s8 = [r for r in rows if r["run"].replace("pois200_", "") in eight]
        k8 = lambda key: sorted(100.0 * r[key] / r["blocked"] for r in s8)
        v8, p8 = k8("valve"), k8("over")
        print("八条非重复 Foyer 臂（正文用的集合）：阀门中位 %.1f%%、投影超预算中位 %.1f%%"
              % (mid(v8), mid(p8)))
        # 两种中位口径（上中位 vs 两数均值）在八条臂上的差 —— 正文 §V 专门有一段
        # 「约定说明」，引的就是这两个数；原来它们只在正文里，文件里没有。
        def mean2(v):
            v = sorted(v); n = len(v)
            return (v[n // 2 - 1] + v[n // 2]) / 2 if n % 2 == 0 else v[n // 2]
        print("两种中位口径在八条臂上的差：投影 %.1fpp、阀门 %.1fpp"
              % (mid(p8) - mean2(p8), mid(v8) - mean2(v8)))
        # 【全表】的上中位也要落成一行：正文写「over every run in that file they are
        # 27.0% and 5.4%」，这两个数【不在】前面任何一行里（eight_* 是八条臂、
        # paper_set_* 是十条），只能从逐行重算 —— 那就不满足「数字要能追到文件」。
        va = sorted(100.0 * r["valve"] / r["blocked"] for r in rows)
        pa = sorted(100.0 * r["over"] / r["blocked"] for r in rows)
        ra = sorted(100.0 * r["rem"] / r["blocked"] for r in rows)
        print("全表（%d 条）：阀门上中位 %.1f%%、投影超预算上中位 %.1f%%、remainder 上中位 %.1f%%"
              % (len(rows), mid(va), mid(pa), mid(ra)))
        with open(out_path, "a") as f:
            f.write("# all_rows_median（%d 条，正文「over every run in that file」用的就是这一行）,"
                    "valve_pct=%.1f,projection_over_pct=%.1f,remainder_pct=%.1f\n"
                    % (len(rows), mid(va), mid(pa), mid(ra)))
        with open(out_path, "a") as f:
            f.write("# 正文用的集合（八条非重复 Foyer 臂）：%s\n" % " ".join(sorted(
                r["run"] for r in s8)))
            f.write("# eight_median,valve_pct=%.1f,projection_over_pct=%.1f\n"
                    % (mid(v8), mid(p8)))
            f.write("# eight_range,valve=%.1f~%.1f,projection_over=%.1f~%.1f\n"
                    % (v8[0], v8[-1], p8[0], p8[-1]))
            f.write("# convention_delta（上中位 − 两数均值，八条臂）,"
                    "projection_pp=%.1f,valve_pp=%.1f\n"
                    % (mid(p8) - mean2(p8), mid(v8) - mean2(v8)))
            f.write("# 论文正文引用的集合：%s\n" % " ".join(sorted(r["run"] for r in sel)))
            f.write("# paper_set_median,valve_pct=%.1f,projection_over_pct=%.1f,remainder_pct=%.1f\n"
                    % (mid(vp), mid(op), mid(rp)))
            f.write("# paper_set_range,valve=%.1f~%.1f,projection_over=%.1f~%.1f\n"
                    % (vp[0], vp[-1], op[0], op[-1]))
    print("-> %s" % out_path)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""控制器的占用投影 —— 论文 §V「控制器以为快满了」那句话的唯一数据来源。

为什么必须单独立一个脚本：原来那个 89,073 是手算的，复现不出来。核实时还发现
日志里的 `base` 字段【不可信】—— controller_budget3.py 在候选循环里重绑了
`base = s["ctx"] or s["prompt0"]`（候选者的上下文），而写日志是在循环【之后】，
所以有候选被尝试的周期里，日志里的 base 是候选者的值，不是投影的地板。

投影的正确算法（controller_budget3.py:321-326）：
    resident   = floor * pool_tokens      # 引擎设备侧占用
    ctx_sum    = sum(活跃会话的 ctx 或 prompt0)
    growth_sum = sum(forecast(s) for s in active)
    projection = max(resident, ctx_sum) + growth_sum

本脚本用 ctx_sum / resident / growth_sum 三个【独立记录】的字段重算投影，
绕开被污染的 base。

用法: gen_projection.py <results_dir> <out.csv>

**必须在实验机上跑**：本表统计【所有】带 controller.jsonl 的 run。本地镜像是部分
快照（2026-09-24：本地 34 行 vs 实验机 47 行），在本地跑会把正文引用的区间与中位数
算错 —— 正文原写「1.24 to 2.00 / median 1.75 / over every run 1.01 to 4.11 / 1.35」，
在 47 行的实验机版本上是 1.24--2.00（中位 1.78）、1.01--4.11（中位 1.55）。
见 CLAUDE.md「本地镜像是部分快照」。下面有 MIN_ROWS 断言。
"""
import csv, glob, json, os, statistics, sys

def med(v):
    v = sorted(v)
    return v[len(v) // 2] if v else None

def main():
    root = sys.argv[1]
    out = sys.argv[2]
    rows = []
    for p in sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(root, "*", "controller.jsonl"))):
        run = os.path.basename(os.path.dirname(p))
        if run.startswith("_"):
            continue
        L = []
        for line in open(p):
            line = line.strip()
            if not line:
                continue
            try:
                d = json.loads(line)
            except ValueError:
                continue
            if "budget" in d and "ctx_sum" in d and "growth_sum" in d:
                L.append(d)
        if len(L) < 200:
            continue
        b = L[-1]["budget"]
        proj = [max(d.get("resident") or 0, d.get("ctx_sum") or 0) + (d.get("growth_sum") or 0)
                for d in L]
        occ = [d.get("resident") or 0 for d in L]
        pm, om = med(proj), med(occ)
        rows.append(dict(run=run, samples=len(L), budget=b,
                         proj_p50=pm, occ_p50=om,
                         # growth_p50：增长预留单独的中位。论文 §V 的「growth reserve
                         # alone is 26,762 tokens, 28% of the budget」用的就是它 ——
                         # 2026-09-24 审稿人指出这个数在文件里查不到（能复算，但没落盘），
                         # 于是加列。注意是【全部周期的中位】，不是只看有准入/有等待的周期。
                         growth_p50=med([d.get("growth_sum") or 0 for d in L]),
                         proj_pct=100.0 * pm / b, occ_pct=100.0 * om / b,
                         # free = 预算里还空着的百分比。论文 §V 的「7.6% free」
                         # 就是这个数 —— 此前不落盘，审计报成找不到源头。
                         free_pct=100.0 - 100.0 * pm / b,
                         ratio=(pm / om) if om else float("nan")))
    # 镜像完整性护栏：本表是【对 run 集合】取区间与中位数，少 run 就少样本、数字静默变。
    # 断言必须落在【产出行数】上，不能落在文件数上 —— 本地有 63 个 controller.jsonl
    # （实验机 83），可用行却只有 34（实验机 47）：按文件数设阈值根本拦不住
    # （2026-09-24 第一次就设错了，本地照样跑完并写出了偏小的区间）。
    assert len(rows) >= 45, (
        "只产出 %d 行（实验机 47 行）—— 这多半是部分镜像，本脚本必须在实验机上跑。" % len(rows))
    rows.sort(key=lambda r: -r["ratio"])
    print("%-26s %7s %9s %9s %9s %8s" % ("run", "采样", "投影中位", "占用中位", "投影%", "倍数"))
    for r in rows:
        print("%-26s %7d %9.0f %9.0f %8.1f%% %8.2f"
              % (r["run"], r["samples"], r["proj_p50"], r["occ_p50"], r["proj_pct"], r["ratio"]))
    with open(out, "w") as f:
        f.write("# 控制器占用投影 —— gen_projection.py 生成\n")
        f.write("# projection = max(resident, ctx_sum) + growth_sum（绕开被候选循环重绑的 base 字段）\n")
        f.write("# growth_p50 = 全部周期上 growth_sum 的中位（正文 §V 的 26,762 tokens 即 foyer_hc 的该列）\n")
        f.write("run,samples,budget,proj_p50,occ_p50,growth_p50,proj_pct_of_budget,"
                "occ_pct_of_budget,free_pct,ratio\n")
        for r in rows:
            f.write("%s,%d,%d,%.0f,%.0f,%.0f,%.1f,%.1f,%.1f,%.2f\n" % (
                r["run"], r["samples"], r["budget"], r["proj_p50"], r["occ_p50"],
                r["growth_p50"], r["proj_pct"], r["occ_pct"], r["free_pct"], r["ratio"]))
    print("-> %s" % out)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""25 档同代对照表（论文表 II）—— paper/data/tier25.csv 的生成器。

这张表原来是 2026-09-23 从实验机 run_summary.py 的打印输出【手工导出】的，
仓库里没有生成器。「每个数字都能追到脚本」在这张表上原本是打折扣的。

本脚本直接 **import run_summary.py 并调用它的 summary()**，不复制实现 ——
口径（墙钟 / tw_conc / SLO / 段 SD / TTFT 中位）与实验机逐字相同，
包括那个容易踩的点：段数 nseg = max(2, min(10, 完成步数 // 20))，
所以 125 步是 **6 段**而不是 10 段（表 II 的 Seg. SD 列就是这个口径）。

用法: python3 gen_tier25.py <results_dir> <out.csv>
"""
import csv, importlib.util, os, sys

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
# 表 II 的四条臂；name 是表里印的名字，run 是目录名
ARMS = [("cap2_25", "pois200_cap2_25", "static=2"),
        ("cap3_25", "pois200_cap3_25", "static=3"),
        ("cap4_25", "pois200_cap4_25", "static=4"),
        ("foyer_push_25", "pois200_foyer_push_25", "budget3:hw=0;target=0.95")]


def rs_mod():
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("_rs25", os.path.join(HERE, "run_summary.py"))
    m = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(m)
    return m


def main():
    root, out_p = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
    RS = rs_mod()
    RS.R = root
    rows = []
    print("%-16s %5s %9s %7s %7s %8s %9s" % ("run", "步数", "墙钟min", "Concurrency", "SLO%", "SegSDpp", "TTFT中位ms"))
    for name, run, policy in ARMS:
        d, err = RS.summary(run)
        if not d:
            raise SystemExit("!! %s 读不到：%s —— 缺 run 不静默跳过" % (run, err))
        if d["steps"] != 125:
            raise SystemExit("!! %s 只有 %d 步（25 档应为 125）" % (run, d["steps"]))
        rows.append(dict(run=name, steps=d["steps"], wall_min=round(d["wall"], 1),
                         conc=round(d["conc"], 2), slo_pct=round(d["slo"], 1),
                         seg_sd_pp=round(d["sd"], 1), ttft_p50_ms=round(d["ttft"]),
                         policy=policy))
        print("%-16s %5d %9.4f %7.2f %7.1f %8.1f %9.0f" % (
            name, d["steps"], d["wall"], d["conc"], d["slo"], d["sd"], d["ttft"]))
    hdr = ["# 25 档（125 步）同代 harness 对照 —— 由 scripts/lab/gen_tier25.py 生成，勿手改",
           "# 口径与实验机的 run_summary.py 逐字相同（本脚本直接 import 它，不复制实现）。",
           "# ⚠ 段数 nseg = max(2, min(10, 完成步数//20))：125 步是 6 段，999 步才是 10 段，",
           "#   所以本表的 Seg. SD 与表 I 的 sd_pp 不是同一个段数，不可直接比。",
           "# 四条臂的 code hash 相同（同一代 harness）；cap4 与 Foyer 臂是为本次修订补跑的。",
           "run,steps,wall_min,conc,slo_pct,seg_sd_pp,ttft_p50_ms,policy"]
    body = "\n".join(hdr) + "\n" + "\n".join(
        "%s,%d,%.1f,%.2f,%.1f,%.1f,%d,%s" % (r["run"], r["steps"], r["wall_min"], r["conc"],
                                             r["slo_pct"], r["seg_sd_pp"], r["ttft_p50_ms"],
                                             r["policy"]) for r in rows) + "\n"
    assert isinstance(body, str)
    with open(out_p, "w") as f:
        f.write(body)
    print("-> %s（%d 行）" % (out_p, len(rows)))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

configuration holds nearly half the pool idle (48.2\% of its budget, 50.8\% of\
the pool) with 66 sessions queued, because
it believes it has 7.6\% free when it has 48.2\%. Any account of why it is slow
that does not mention this is incomplete.

We then asked what actually stops it, by instrumenting the admission decision.
The path is a conjunction: a session is authorized only if the SLO valve is
open, the round gate is not full, and its projected footprint fits what remains
of the budget. Over the base run's 8{,}196 blocked cycles---cycles with a
session waiting and none admitted---the valve accounts for 6.8\%, the round gate
for none, and a projection already above budget for 38.4\%.

The remaining 54.8\% are cycles where the controller selected no candidate at
all, and here we are explicit about the limit of what we can resolve. The
controller is single-flight by default: it authorizes at most one session per
two-second cycle and waits for that session to begin a round before authorizing
the next. Whether that gate held or whether a candidate was tried and did not
fit is \emph{not distinguishable from our logs}---the controller does not record
it---so we do not attribute the bucket to either. We bounded it by running that arm: the same configuration with single-flight
disabled. Two things came out of it, and the second is the one that matters.

The bound first. With the gate off, the residual bucket---which can then only
be \emph{candidates that did not fit}, since the gate cannot be holding---falls
from 54.8\% of blocked cycles to 39.7\%, against 44.0\% that are over budget
outright. So the single-flight path accounts for roughly fifteen points, and
the headroom estimate remains the largest single cause. (Both arms' numbers
are rows of \texttt{gate\_attrib.csv}, which now includes the \texttt{sf=0}
arm; an earlier revision quoted 39.9 and 43.5 for the second pair, values that
appeared in no data file.)
We report what did \emph{not} move, because it is the check we wrote down for
this probe before running it. The two aggregate indicators barely did:
admitting cycles go from 2.35\% to 2.44\% and blocked cycles from 93.3\% to
93.2\%, so on those two the flag looks like it changed nothing. What moves is
the composition \emph{within} the blocked cycles---which is the quantity the
bound above rests on---and it moves well outside the replicate spread, the
repeat of the base arm reading 58.3\% remainder against the original's 54.8\%.
So the probe supports the decomposition and not the claim that disabling the
gate makes the controller admit appreciably more, which is what we say above
it buys 28 minutes for 3.5\,pp rather than a win.

The second thing is that disabling it does not make the controller win.
works; it is evidence that the question needs more runs than we have. We state
the conditional version in the abstract, and we flag this as the second thing
we would re-run with more GPU time.

\paragraph{The SLO metric excludes the controller's own cost, and we checked.}
Every SLO number above counts a request as good if its time-to-first-token is
under 10\,s, and the runner measures that from the moment the request is
\emph{submitted to the engine}. A session that waits for a permit has not been
submitted yet, so the wait is invisible---and that wait is precisely the cost
an admission controller imposes. We therefore recomputed the runs we have per-step
logs for on a ready-to-first-token basis, where a round's clock starts when the
session is actually able to run (its arrival for round~0, the previous round's
completion plus its tool wait otherwise). That is nineteen of the twenty-five
200-session runs in Table~\ref{tab:runs}; absent are the no-control baseline,
the two HiCache anchor repeats and three controller repeats, and we do not
extend the claim to them.

The correction is large. Every configuration loses 2.9--19.3\,pp of SLO, and the
loss is systematic: tight caps queue the hardest, so \texttt{cap1} falls from
94.8\% to 76.2\% while \texttt{cap5} falls only from 27.1\% to 24.2\%. A
session admitted last under \texttt{cap1} can wait hours for its turn; its
engine-side TTFT is still 1.6\,s.

What does \emph{not} change is the verdict, within the subset we recomputed.
The static envelope keeps the same four anchors---the two anchor
repeats, which we did not recompute on the ready clock, stand in as their first
runs, and every distance below carries that substitution
(\texttt{arrival\_slo.csv} records the columns)---and of the eleven controller
arms, nine move by no more than
2.5\,pp and none crosses the $\pm1.0$\,pp band into a new verdict. Two move
more: the all-throttles-off arm improves by 8.2\,pp and the retuned AIMD arm by
3.2\,pp, both of which stay below the front either way. The one that decides our headline moves the wrong way for
the controller: on the ready clock \texttt{horizon}=1's own SLO falls from
84.8\% to 68.2\% while the anchor it is measured against falls less, so the
ready clock moves it further below the line rather than closer to it. We do not
give a ready-clock distance for it, because the two anchor repeats that define
the line are not in the set we could recompute (\S\ref{sec:discussion} says
which runs are), and inventing a line for them would be the same error this
subsection is about.

The subset matters and we state its edge. The two configurations whose repeats
decide how we read the verdict---\texttt{horizon}=1's repeat and the fastest
anchor \texttt{cap4}+HC's---are not in the nineteen, because we did not
recompute their per-step logs on the ready clock; the rebuilt envelope
therefore rests on the original anchors without their repeats, and a
ready-clock frontier that included them could move the line by the 3.9 and
5.1\,pp those repeats differ by. We do not claim the verdict is unchanged under
every SLO definition---only that on the runs we could recompute, moving the
clock to the ready point costs every configuration 3--19\,pp and changes no
verdict in that set.
We report this because a reviewer should ask it,
and because the answer is the useful part: the negative result is not an
artifact of choosing an SLO definition that flatters controllers. If anything
the flattering choice was the one that moved the arm nearest the line from
$-0.9$ to $+1.5$---that is, from just below the line to just above the tie
band's upper edge, by a margin of the same order as that pair's own replicate

# 25 档（125 步）同代 harness 对照 —— 由 scripts/lab/gen_tier25.py 生成，勿手改
# 口径与实验机的 run_summary.py 逐字相同（本脚本直接 import 它，不复制实现）。
# ⚠ 段数 nseg = max(2, min(10, 完成步数//20))：125 步是 6 段，999 步才是 10 段，
#   所以本表的 Seg. SD 与表 I 的 sd_pp 不是同一个段数，不可直接比。
# 四条臂的 code hash 相同（同一代 harness）；cap4 与 Foyer 臂是为本次修订补跑的。
run,steps,wall_min,conc,slo_pct,seg_sd_pp,ttft_p50_ms,policy
cap2_25,125,32.4,1.95,92.8,11.0,1409,static=2
cap3_25,125,24.2,2.77,91.2,5.1,1568,static=3
cap4_25,125,23.6,3.39,79.2,21.4,3030,static=4

# trace: replay_pois200_l04.csv（999 行 - 200 会话 = 799 次转换）
# 轮次间隔：trace 要求 vs 实测 —— 由 scripts/lab/gen_toolwait.py 生成，勿手改
# 轮次转换 = 同会话相邻两轮 = 每会话除最后一轮外的每一行
# ⚠ 分母不同，均值差很多，正文引用时必须指明口径：

def tw_conc(d):
    p = os.path.join(d, "admissions.jsonl")
    if not os.path.exists(p):
        return None
    ev = []
    for ln in open(p):
        ln = ln.strip()
        if not ln:
            continue
        try:
            e = json.loads(ln)
        except ValueError:
            continue
        k = {"admit": 1, "release": -1, "resume": 1}.get(e.get("event"))
        if k and e.get("ts") is not None:
            ev.append((float(e["ts"]), k))
    if len(ev) < 2:
        return None
    ev.sort()
    area, cur = 0.0, 0
    for i, (t, k) in enumerate(ev):
        if i:
            area += cur * (t - ev[i - 1][0])
        cur += k
    sp = ev[-1][0] - ev[0][0]
    return area / sp if sp > 0 else None


def sess_conc(d, rows):
    """没有准入日志时的【会话级】并发 —— 与 tw_conc 同一件事的另一种算法。

    为什么需要：`default`（无准入控制）的 admissions.jsonl 是空的（那个 arm 没有
    控制器，也就没有 admit/release 事件），run_summary.py 的 tw_conc 对它返回 None。
    2026-09-24 发现 runs.csv 里那一格被填成了 **1.69**，而 1.69 是
    metrics.csv 的 sglang:num_running_reqs 全时段均值 —— 也就是表 I 标题里
    明确说「Conc. 不是它」的那个量。同一列在 27 行是一个口径、在 1 行是另一个口径，
    而且是无声的。

    这个估计量 = Σ(每个会话的 first submit → last complete) / 全体跨度，
    与 tw_conc 的定义（面积/跨度）同构。**在四条静态 cap 上与 tw_conc 逐位相符**
    （cap1 0.999 vs 1.00、cap2 1.990 vs 1.99、cap5 4.895 vs 4.89、cap2_25 1.954 vs 1.95），
    在控制器臂上略高（控制器释放 permit 早于会话最后一步，foyer_hc 2.11 vs 2.06），
    所以它是可比但不等价的替代 —— 只在 tw_conc 无值时使用，并在表里注明。
    """
    S = {}
    for r in rows:
        if r.get("submit_timestamp") and r.get("complete_timestamp"):
            k = r["session_id"]; a, b = r["submit_timestamp"], r["complete_timestamp"]
            if k in S:
                S[k][0] = min(S[k][0], a); S[k][1] = max(S[k][1], b)
            else:
                S[k] = [a, b]
    if not S:
        return None
    lo = min(v[0] for v in S.values()); hi = max(v[1] for v in S.values())
    return sum(b - a for a, b in S.values()) / (hi - lo) if hi > lo else None


def one(root, name):
    d = os.path.join(root, "pois200_" + name)
    sp = os.path.join(d, "steps.jsonl")
    if not os.path.exists(sp):
        raise SystemExit("!! %s 没有 steps.jsonl（目录 %s）—— 缺 run 时不静默跳过" % (name, d))
    rows = []
    for ln in open(sp):
        ln = ln.strip()
        if not ln:
            continue
        try:
            r = json.loads(ln)
        except ValueError:
            continue
        if r.get("complete_timestamp"):
            rows.append(r)
    rows.sort(key=lambda r: r["complete_timestamp"])
    tt = [r["first_token_ms"] for r in rows if r.get("first_token_ms") is not None]
    nseg = max(2, min(10, len(rows) // 20))
    seg = []
    for k in range(nseg):
        g = rows[k * len(rows) // nseg:(k + 1) * len(rows) // nseg]
        h = [y["first_token_ms"] for y in g if y.get("first_token_ms") is not None]
        if h:
            seg.append(100 * sum(1 for t in h if t < 10000) / len(h))
    c, src = tw_conc(d), "admission log"
    if c is None:
        # 只在没有准入日志时才退回会话级估计，并记录来源（见 sess_conc 的注释）
        c, src = sess_conc(d, rows), "session spans (no admission log)"
    return dict(
        run=name, steps=len(rows),
        wall=(max(r["complete_timestamp"] for r in rows)
              - min(r["submit_timestamp"] for r in rows if r.get("submit_timestamp"))) / 60.0,
        conc=c, conc_src=src,
        slo=100 * sum(1 for t in tt if t < 10000) / len(tt),
        sd=st.pstdev(seg) if len(seg) > 1 else 0.0,
        nseg=nseg)


#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""任意长度的 run 都能量：墙钟 / 并发 / SLO / 命中 / run 内稳定性。

为什么需要它。现有脚本（fig_poisson_status.py 等）都写死了"必须跑满 999 步"，
于是短 trace（r2 = 399 步）的 run 会被静默跳过——而短 trace 正是快速迭代要用的。
这个脚本不假设长度，段数按实际步数除以十取整，短 run 也不会崩。

    run_summary.py <run 名> [<run 名> ...]
    run_summary.py --prefix pois200_          # 列出所有匹配的
"""
import json, os, statistics, sys

R = "/data/xbw/turnstile/results/night"
POOL = 101432


def tw_conc(D):
    """时间加权平均并发（不是按 admit 事件数加权——那会被高并发时刻带偏）。"""
    ev = []
    p = D + "/admissions.jsonl"
    if not os.path.exists(p):
        return None
    for ln in open(p):
        ln = ln.strip()
        if not ln:
            continue
        try:
            e = json.loads(ln)
        except ValueError:
            continue
        d = {"admit": 1, "release": -1, "resume": 1}.get(e.get("event"))
        if d and e.get("ts") is not None:
            ev.append((float(e["ts"]), d))
    if len(ev) < 2:
        return None
    ev.sort()
    area = 0.0
    cur = 0
    for i, (t, d) in enumerate(ev):
        if i > 0:
            area += cur * (t - ev[i - 1][0])
        cur += d
    sp = ev[-1][0] - ev[0][0]
    return area / sp if sp > 0 else None


def summary(nm):
    D = os.path.join(R, nm)
    if not os.path.isdir(D):
        return None, "目录不存在"
    try:
        m = json.load(open(D + "/metadata.json"))
        b = m.get("build") or {}
    except Exception as e:
        return None, "metadata 读不到: %s" % e
    st = []
    try:
        for ln in open(D + "/steps.jsonl"):
            ln = ln.strip()
            if not ln:
                continue
            try:
                r = json.loads(ln)
            except ValueError:
                continue

\paragraph{Where the mechanism stops.} With HiCache enabled the
correspondence breaks---and it breaks in a way that confirms the mechanism
rather than undermining it. The device cache misses as much as before:
\texttt{cap4\_hc} misses 22.4\,M tokens on the device, more than the
device-only \texttt{cap4} misses. But only 9.1\,M of that is recomputed; the
other 13.3\,M is served from the host tier. Across the nineteen host-tier
runs the host serves 9.5--19.3\,M tokens each, and across the five device-only
runs it serves almost nothing---0.006\% to 0.25\% of the device miss, the rest
of which is recomputed on all five. Recomputation is still what the device cannot
absorb; the host tier is a second absorber in front of it.

\subsection{The tier, not the policy, decides whether reuse survives}

The device-miss column hides the quantity that matters. Define
\emph{reuse} as the prompt tokens served from anywhere in the memory
hierarchy---device radix cache plus host tier---so that
$\text{reuse} + \text{recomputed} = \text{prompt}$, with the prompt fixed at
28.5\,M by the trace.

The bound on reuse has to be defined independently of what we observed,
or the argument is circular. We take it from the trace: summing the planned
reusable prefix (\texttt{prefix\_len}) over all 999 rounds of
\texttt{replay\_pois200\_l04.csv} gives 21{,}540{,}371 tokens against a prompt
total of 28{,}526{,}871, so no policy can reuse more than 75.5\% of the
prompt. That is a property of the workload. It is also a bound rather than a
description: \emph{no configuration we ran reaches it}, and the best realizes
92.5\%, so the gap between ``planned'' and ``achieved'' exists even in the
easiest case. What varies across configurations is how much of the bound is
realized, and that is where the tier enters.

\begin{center}
% 这张表在 conference 模板下比单栏宽 56.9pt（Overfull 会一直挂在编译日志里）。
% \small + tabcolsep 3pt 后 244.4pt < 252pt。改列宽不是改数字。
\small\setlength{\tabcolsep}{3pt}%
\begin{tabular}{lcccccc}
\toprule
device-only & $\overline{R}_{\text{busy}}$ & 1.00 & 1.27 & 1.53 & 1.65 & 1.84 \\
            & realized (\% of bound) & 92.5 & 89.7 & 79.0 & 60.7 & 36.4 \\
            & recomputed (M)         &  8.6 &  9.2 & 11.5 & 15.4 & 20.7 \\
\midrule
host tier   & $\overline{R}_{\text{busy}}$ & 1.25 & 1.51 & 1.73 & & \\
            & realized (\% of bound) & 92.4 & 91.3 & 90.2 & & \\
            & recomputed (M)         &  8.6 &  8.9 &  9.1 & & \\
\bottomrule
\end{tabular}
\end{center}

Device-only, realization falls from 92.5\% to 36.4\% and the shortfall is
recomputation: 8.6\,M to 20.7\,M tokens. With a host tier it holds at
90.2--92.4\% on the static points that span the same range, and at
72.8--92.5\% across the nineteen host-tier runs including every controller
arm---the lowest being the arm that disables every throttle and therefore runs
the pool hardest. (A twentieth host-tier run, the \texttt{horizon}=1 repeat, is
excluded from this accounting: its engine prompt-token counter reads
$1.41\times$ the trace plan with zero retractions and no client-side retries,
and we could not reconcile it.) It holds
while the \emph{device} misses more than the device-only family's at every
point (21.6--27.9\,M against 8.6--20.7\,M), because the host serves
9.5--19.3\,M in front of the recomputation instead of letting the device keep
it. A reader watching only the device hit rate would conclude the host tier
made things worse. (Two denominators appear here and we name them: the 75.5\%
is the trace's bound as a share of the prompt, and 92.5\% is the best observed
reuse as a share of \emph{that bound}---the same run reuses 69.9\% of its
prompt. \texttt{reuse.csv} carries \texttt{reuse\_frac} and
\texttt{realized\_frac} side by side so the two cannot be confused.)

This is the same collapse \S\ref{sec:related} reports under a different
normalization. That section quotes the round-1-and-later prefix hit rate---%
steps restricted to rounds $\ge 1$, unweighted mean over steps---which falls

Before the routes, one measurement that reframes all of them. Our controller
keeps every session's permit for its lifetime, so its pool occupancy is a
policy output, not a constraint. It runs at a median of 49.2\% of the pool on
the base configuration, and the median number of sessions queueing for
admission is 66. The configuration that finishes fastest---the repeat of that same static cap
of four, at 209.1 minutes---runs at 71.9\%, and its first run at 70.2\%. Occupancy
and running requests track each other reliably across the arms we have (the two
columns in \texttt{occupancy.csv} rank the twenty-three arms we have almost
identically, Spearman $\rho=0.95$), but
running count does \emph{not} order wall clock: \texttt{horizon}=2 runs 1.25
requests against the base arm's 1.14 (the all-samples mean, the column
\texttt{occupancy.csv} carries; \S\ref{sec:model} uses the busy-conditional
one) and finishes 15 minutes slower. That gap is
what the next subsection is about.

The controller believes it is nearly full. Its own decision log records the
projection it admits against---the largest of measured occupancy and the summed
contexts of live sessions, plus a reserve for their growth---at a median of
89{,}073 tokens against a budget of 96{,}360, i.e.\ 7.6\% free. Its measured
occupancy at the same instants is 49{,}875 tokens: 48.2\% of that budget, or
50.8\% of the pool, is idle. The
projection is 1.79$\times$ the truth on this configuration. Across the
twenty controller runs that logged projections on this trace the ratio runs
from 1.24 to 2.00 with a median of 1.78, and from 1.24 to 4.11 with the same
median once the second trace's controller arm is included
(\texttt{projection.csv}; the second trace is the 4.11). So 1.79 is this arm's
value, not a constant of the design, and we name the set because the range
depends on it---over every run in that file, including the flash-crowd and
25-session families, it is 1.01 to 4.11 with a median of 1.55. Medians here are
the upper of the two middle values, the convention \S\ref{sec:results:attribution}
comparison, and a reviewer should read it that way. The same-generation
25-session measurement we do have is in Table~\ref{tab:tier25}, and it does not
contain that arm.

This is the regime in which adaptive control should win, and we now have the
same-generation measurement that earlier drafts of this paper were missing.
Four arms on the 25-session tier, all under the current harness
(Table~\ref{tab:tier25}): static caps of two, three and four finish in 32.4,
24.2 and 23.6 minutes at 92.8\%, 91.2\% and 79.2\% SLO, and \sys{} finishes
in 27.6 minutes at 92.8\%.

  \caption{The 25-session tier, all four arms under the current harness.
  This is the same-generation comparison earlier drafts of this paper were
  missing; \texttt{cap4} and the \sys{} arm were run for this revision.}

  \centering
  \small
  \setlength{\tabcolsep}{4pt}

  \end{tabular}
\end{table}

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""生成 paper/data/eviction.csv —— 论文 §3.3 里「驱逐 → 重算」那条因果链的数据。

为什么要有这个脚本：这条链子曾经被错误地"反驳"过，用的两个检验都是恒等式：

  1. "total prefilled tokens == planned prefill, ratio 1.00"
     `sglang:prompt_tokens_total` 计的是**完整提交的 prompt 长度，含 cached 部分**，
     所以它恒等于 sum(prompt_len)，不管重算多少都不会变。这是个恒等式。
     能测出重算的是 `server_uncached_prompt_tokens`，本脚本报的就是它。

  2. "SGLang reports zero retractions"
     radix cache 驱逐记在 `sglang:evicted_tokens_total`；retraction 指的是
     抢占**正在跑**的请求，是另一件事、另一个计数器。拿后者反驳前者是范畴错误。

正确的读法（本脚本的输出）：
  - 设备侧未命中 = 设备没接住的量（prompt − cached_tokens_total）
  - 重算量 = steps 里的 server_uncached_prompt_tokens（真正重新 prefill 的）
  - 两者之差 = host 梯队接住的量
  - 无 HiCache 的五条：host 接住【精确为 0】，设备未命中 == 重算量，1:1
  - 有 HiCache 的八条：host 接住 13~19M
  - 但 HiCache 那一族的 τ 仍然散开（重算恒定 8.7M，τ 22.5~30.6ms）→ 未解释

用法: python3 gen_eviction.py <results_dir> <out.csv> [run ...]
"""
import csv, json, os, statistics, sys, glob

HEADER = ["run", "hicache", "prompt_M", "dev_miss_M", "host_hit_M", "uncached_M",
          "evicted_M", "running", "step_back_ms", "step_perreq_ms",
          # 下面三列是【未舍入】的原始计数，专供下游算比例用。
          # 为什么必须单列：曾经让下游拿舍入到 0.1M 的列去算百分比，cap1 于是印成
          # 69.8% 而真值是 19,934,847/28,526,871 = 69.9%（2026-09-24 审稿人从原始
          # 日志独立求和抓到的）。凡是要算比例的，一律走这三列。
          "prompt_tok", "reuse_tok", "uncached_tok",
          "dev_miss_tok", "host_hit_tok"]
SAMPLE_S = 5.0

RUNS = ["cap1", "cap2", "cap3_fixed", "cap4", "cap5", "pairB_cap2hc", "cap3_hc",
        "cap4_hc", "foyer_hc", "foyer_hz1", "foyer_hz2", "foyer_hz0", "foyer_bres"]


def last_value(path, name):
    """取某一列最后一个有效值（这些是累计计数器）。"""
    hdr = open(path).readline().strip().split(",")
    if name not in hdr:
        return None
    i = hdr.index(name)
    v = None
    for line in open(path).readlines()[1:]:
        c = line.rstrip("\n").split(",")
        if len(c) <= i:
            continue
        try:
            v = float(c[i])
        except ValueError:
            pass
    return v


def col(path, name):
    hdr = open(path).readline().strip().split(",")
    if name not in hdr:
        return []
    i = hdr.index(name)
    out = []
    for line in open(path).readlines()[1:]:
        c = line.rstrip("\n").split(",")
        if len(c) <= i:
            continue
        try:
            out.append(float(c[i]))
        except ValueError:
            pass
    return out


def main():
    root, out_path = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
    runs = sys.argv[3:] or RUNS
    rows, bad, missing = [], [], []
    for r in runs:
        d = os.path.join(root, "pois200_" + r)
        mp = os.path.join(d, "metrics.csv")
        sp = os.path.join(d, "steps.jsonl")
        if not (os.path.exists(mp) and os.path.exists(sp)):
            missing.append(r)
            continue
        try:
            # ⚠ prompt 必须与 uncached **同源**，否则 reuse = prompt - uncached 是拿
            # 两个不同来源相减。原来这里取 metrics.csv 的 sglang:prompt_tokens_total，
            # 而 uncached 是 steps.jsonl 的求和 —— cap3_fixed 上两者差 43,112 tokens
            # （0.15%），兑现率因此从 78.9% 变 79.0%（2026-09-24 审稿人抓）。
            # 下面的断言 1 本来就要求 prompt == 计划 prefill（1% 内），直接用它即可。
            steps = [json.loads(l) for l in open(sp) if l.strip()]
            prompt = sum(s.get("prompt_len") or 0 for s in steps)
            cached = last_value(mp, "sglang:cached_tokens_total")
            evicted = last_value(mp, "sglang:evicted_tokens_total")
            uncached = sum(s.get("server_uncached_prompt_tokens") or 0 for s in steps)
            plan = prompt

            # 断言 1：prompt_tokens_total 必须等于计划 prefill —— 这是恒等式，
            # 挡住的是列读错（读错列不会有这个性质）
            _mp_tot = last_value(mp, "sglang:prompt_tokens_total")
            assert _mp_tot and abs(_mp_tot - plan) / plan < 0.01, \
                "prompt_tokens_total %.1fM 与计划 %.1fM 不符，列可能读错了" % (
                    (_mp_tot or 0) / 1e6, plan / 1e6)

            # 断言 2：cached 必须是 prompt 的一部分（不是独立的量）
            assert cached is not None and 0 <= cached <= prompt * 1.01, \
                "cached_tokens_total 不在 [0, prompt] 内"
            # 注意：prompt − cached == steps 的未命中数，这条【只在无 HiCache 时成立】。
            # 有 HiCache 时两者差很多（cap4_hc: 22.4M vs 9.1M），因为
            # sglang:cached_tokens_total 只计【设备侧】radix cache 的命中，
            # host 梯队接住的那部分不进这个计数器，但逐请求 usage 里算作 cached。
            # 所以本表以 steps 的未命中数为准（那才是真正重新 prefill 的量），
            # 并把设备侧命中单列出来，两者的差就是 host 梯队的贡献。
            dev_miss = prompt - cached   # 设备侧未命中

            rn = col(mp, "sglang:num_running_reqs")
            # HiCache 判定：优先读 metadata 的 build.hicache_enabled（新 run 都有），
            # 读不到再退回硬编码集合。**不能只靠硬编码** —— 原来那份漏了
            # foyer_breshz1（它 host 接住 14.4M，确实是 host-tier run），
            # 于是 §III 的"八条 host-tier run"其实是九条，报的区间是从少一个
            # 成员的集合算的，而漏掉的那个恰是最极端的一点（2026-09-24 审稿人抓）。
            HC_FALLBACK = {"pairB_cap2hc", "cap3_hc", "cap4_hc", "foyer_hc",
                           "foyer_hz1", "foyer_hz2", "foyer_hz0", "foyer_bres",
                           "foyer_breshz1"}
            _mj = os.path.join(d, "metadata.json")
            hc_flag = None
            if os.path.exists(_mj):
                try:
                    hc_flag = json.load(open(_mj)).get("build", {}).get("hicache_enabled")
                except Exception:
                    hc_flag = None
            if hc_flag is None:
                hicache = r in HC_FALLBACK
            else:
                hicache = bool(hc_flag)
            rows.append({
                "run": r,
                "hicache": "1" if hicache else "0",
                "prompt_M": round(prompt / 1e6, 1),

                "uncached_M": round(uncached / 1e6, 1),
                "evicted_M": round((evicted or 0) / 1e6, 1),
                "dev_miss_M": round(dev_miss / 1e6, 1),
                "host_hit_M": round((dev_miss - uncached) / 1e6, 1),   # 设备没接住、但也没重算的
                "running": round(statistics.mean(rn), 2) if rn else "",
                "step_back_ms": "", "step_perreq_ms": "",
                "prompt_tok": int(prompt),
                "reuse_tok": int(prompt - uncached),
                "uncached_tok": int(uncached),
                "dev_miss_tok": int(dev_miss),
                "host_hit_tok": int(dev_miss - uncached),
            })
        except Exception as e:
            bad.append((r, str(e)))

    if missing:
        print("!! 以下 run 在 %s 下不存在（镜像不全？）—— 静默丢掉会让数据文件的集合" % root)
        print("!! 与正文引用的集合不一致（2026-09-24 就这样漏掉过 foyer_hz1）：")
        for r in missing:
            print("   %s" % r)
    if bad:
        print("!! 未通过校验：")
        for r, why in bad:
            print("   %-14s %s" % (r, why))

    with open(out_path, "w", newline="") as f:
        f.write("# 驱逐 vs 重算 —— 由 scripts/lab/gen_eviction.py 生成，勿手改\n")
        f.write("# dev_miss_M: prompt - cached_tokens_total —— 【设备侧】没接住的部分\n")
        f.write("# host_hit_M: dev_miss - uncached —— 设备没接住、但也没重算的那部分 = host 梯队接住的。\n#             无 HiCache 时精确为 0（五条全是），有 HiCache 时 13~19M\n")
        f.write("# uncached_M: steps.jsonl 的 server_uncached_prompt_tokens 累加（真正重新 prefill 的量）\n")
        f.write("# evicted_M: metrics.csv 的 sglang:evicted_tokens_total 末值\n")
        f.write("# host_hit_M 是最关键的一列：无 HiCache 的五条精确为 0.0，有 HiCache 的八条 13~19M。\n")
        f.write("# evicted_M 单列出来只作参照——驱逐量本身不决定重算量，dev_miss 才决定。\n")
# 驱逐 vs 重算 —— 由 scripts/lab/gen_eviction.py 生成，勿手改

# host_hit_M: dev_miss - uncached —— 设备没接住、但也没重算的那部分 = host 梯队接住的。
#             无 HiCache 时精确为 0（五条全是），有 HiCache 时 13~19M
# uncached_M: steps.jsonl 的 server_uncached_prompt_tokens 累加（真正重新 prefill 的量）
# evicted_M: metrics.csv 的 sglang:evicted_tokens_total 末值
# host_hit_M 是最关键的一列：无 HiCache 的五条精确为 0.0，有 HiCache 的八条 13~19M。
# evicted_M 单列出来只作参照——驱逐量本身不决定重算量，dev_miss 才决定。
# prompt_tok/reuse_tok/uncached_tok: 未舍入的原始计数，算比例用这三列

\section{Results: Four Routes, One Outcome}
\label{sec:results}

\subsection{The frontier}

Fig.~\ref{fig:frontier} places every completed run on the (wall clock, SLO)
plane and draws the upper-left envelope of the static points. Table~\ref{tab:runs}
gives the numbers and each controller's signed distance from that envelope.

The result is unambiguous. Of nine controller variants, \emph{none} is above
the envelope---not one, on any run, and the pair we repeated most tightly says
so at a resolution the others do not. The closest arm of all is the one with
single-flight admission disabled, at $0.55$ percentage points \emph{below} the
line---inside our $\pm1.0$\,pp tie band, so we record it as \emph{on} the
line rather than below it, but on the below side of it.

The base configuration is the one whose repeats we can trust, and they are
tight: four runs spanning 0.5\,pp of SLO (89.5\% mean, 279.8 to 293.2 minutes),
against the 3.3\,pp that separates it from the single-flight-off arm. So that
difference is several times the noise of its own configuration rather than
inside it, and it is a trade rather than a win: 28 minutes of wall clock bought
with 3.5\,pp of SLO, moving the arm onto the tie band from below it. Not one
configuration in the table gets above the line, and the tightest pair we have
is what makes us willing to say the near misses are misses. A fifth pair, two runs of \texttt{cap3}+HiCache, \emph{is} used, and an
earlier draft of this paper wrongly set it aside saying one of them exceeded
its own cap. That was a different run: the over-cap one is the device-only
\texttt{cap3} repeat (peak in-flight five against a cap of three; at our
300-point in-flight sample grid, 126 samples sit over the cap, a count that
scales with the grid rather than with the run), while both HiCache runs peak at three with none. The
repeat is also both faster and higher on SLO (230.3 minutes, 81.9\% against
236.4 and 81.1\%), so by the same rule that displaced \texttt{cap4}+HiCache's
first run it displaces this one---and moving that anchor is what puts our
best arm below the line rather than on it.\footnote{The repeats are uneven, and
we do not want the tightest pair to stand for all of them. Five configurations
have been run more than once; their SLO spreads are 0.5, 0.8, 1.0, 3.9 and
5.1\,pp (\texttt{replicates.csv}). The two widest belong to the two arms that
decide something---\texttt{horizon}=1 and the leftmost anchor---so where a small
margin matters our repeats are weakest, and where they are strongest (the base
configuration and the \texttt{cap3}+HiCache anchor) the margin is not small. We
report both ends rather than the average.}

The anchor repeats moved the frontier twice and we report both moves. The
leftmost anchor's second run came out both faster (209.1 against 213.0 minutes)
and higher on SLO (68.5 against 63.4), displacing the first; no controller
verdict moves, because every controller finishes to the right of the next
anchor. The middle anchor's repeat moved it the other way for us: it is also
faster and higher, and taking it moves our best arm from \emph{on} the line to
\emph{on the line, below it}. We report every repeat we ran rather than keeping
the better of each pair, because a frontier whose anchors are single
measurements is the weakness this whole subsection is about---and here it is
the weakness biting.

The other end of the front deserves its own sentence, because the rest of this
paper is negative and a reader could close it thinking the answer is to do
nothing. The run with \emph{no} admission control---every session admitted the
moment it arrives---finishes in 288.6 minutes at 3.0\% SLO, $87.6$\,pp below
the envelope and the worst SLO we report. Its median
time-to-first-token is 1{,}774 seconds, its prefix cache hit rate collapses from
the planned 75.5\% the runner prints in its end-of-run summary (the all-steps
convention, a different
one from the round-1+ numbers in \S\ref{sec:related}---for the same trace
\texttt{gen\_nocontrol.py} reports 73.8\% on that convention) to 1.6\%, and its segment SLO is 30\% in the first
tenth of the run and exactly zero in each of the nine after that. Uncontrolled
admission does not degrade gracefully; it destroys the cache and then serves
almost nobody. The question this paper addresses is therefore not whether to
admit selectively, but which selective rule to use.

Two features of the front are worth noting before the negative results.
The envelope is steep between 209 and 230 minutes (0.63\,pp per minute) and
shallow thereafter (0.15\,pp per minute), so the cost of buying SLO by
retreating from the fast end is high. And $W$ is not monotone in concurrency:
five sessions (260.9\,min) is \emph{slower} than four (249.9\,min)---the same
configuration family, no host cache on either side---because the per-step cost
rises faster than the request count does. We return to this
non-monotonicity in \S\ref{sec:model}, where it turns out to be the first
appearance of the mechanism that drives the whole frontier.

\begin{table}[tb]
  \caption{All completed runs. \emph{Conc.} is the time-weighted mean number
  of \emph{live} sessions, which is not the running-request count $\overline{R}$
  used in \S\ref{sec:model}---a live session is parked in a tool wait most of
  the time, so \texttt{cap5} shows 4.89 live against 1.84 running.
  $^\ast$The no-control arm writes no admission log, so its \emph{Conc.} is the
  mean number of live sessions taken from the per-step log's session spans; on
  the four static caps that estimator agrees with the admission-log one to two
  decimals. $\Delta$ is
  the signed distance from the static envelope at that wall clock; above
  $+1.0$\,pp counts as a win, below $-1.0$\,pp as a loss, and the band between
  as a tie (\S\ref{sec:bg}).

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""正文数字 ↔ 数据文件的逐项对照 —— 改完论文后跑一遍。

为什么要有这个：2026-09-24 一天里，每一批修正都引入过被下一轮审查抓到的新错误
（`audit_numbers.py` 只管"这个数在不在任何 CSV 里"，不管"它是否等于 CSV 里那个量
算出来的值"）。本脚本补上后半截：对每一组【有生成器】的派生量，把 CSV 算出来的值
与正文里的串做匹配。

它不是万能的 —— 匹配是字符串级的，正文里一个数可能以 1 位或 2 位小数出现，
所以每个断言都同时试几种写法。**它只回答"正文里有没有这个值"，不回答
"这个值用在这里对不对"**，后者仍然要靠审查。

用法: python3 check_paper_numbers.py          # 全部检查
      python3 check_paper_numbers.py -v       # 连通过的一起列
"""
import csv, glob, os, re, sys

ROOT = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "..", "..")
TEX = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(ROOT, "paper", "*.tex")))
VERBOSE = "-v" in sys.argv


def load(p):
    return [r for r in csv.DictReader(l for l in open(p) if not l.startswith("#"))]


def paper_text():
    return {os.path.basename(f): open(f, encoding="utf-8").read() for f in TEX}


def has(text, v):
    """正文里有没有这个数。试 1~4 位小数、以及千分位两种写法。"""
    s = str(v)
    if "." in s:
        for d in (1, 2, 3, 4):
            t = "%.*f" % (d, float(s))
            if t in text:
                return True
        return False
    n = int(float(s))
    return (str(n) in text or "{:,}".format(n) in text
            or ("{:,}".format(n).replace(",", "{,}")) in text)


def main():
    P = paper_text()
    alltext = "\n".join(P.values())
    fails, secondary, checks = [], [], 0

    def ck(desc, val, where=None, tier="P"):
        """tier="P" 正文引用过这个量，必须出现；tier="S" 论文【不逐条印】这个量
        （只印它的一个聚合/区间），找不到是预期内的，单独列出来供人扫一眼。

        为什么要有这一档：2026-09-24 之前 124 项检查里常年有 20 多项 MISS，
        全是"论文没逐条印"的假阳性 —— 假阳性一多，真 MISS 就淹掉了
        （这正是这个脚本要防的那类错误，只不过发生在脚本自己身上）。
        但 S 档不许变成"眼不见为净"：它照旧逐条打印，只是不计入退出码。
        """
        nonlocal checks
        checks += 1
        src = P.get(where, "") if where else alltext
        if has(src, val):
            if VERBOSE:
                print("  OK   %-58s %s" % (desc, val))
        elif tier == "S":
            secondary.append((desc, val, where or "全篇"))
        else:
            fails.append((desc, val, where or "全篇"))

    # ---- reuse.csv：兑现率 ----
    for r in load(os.path.join(ROOT, "paper/data/reuse.csv")):
        # 兑现率：表 2 印了 device-only 五行与 host tier 三个静态点，其余臂只进区间
        in_tab = r["run"] in ("cap1", "cap2", "cap3_fixed", "cap4", "cap5",
                              "pairB_cap2hc", "cap3_hc", "cap4_hc")
        ck("%s 兑现率" % r["run"], "%.1f" % (float(r["realized_frac"]) * 100),
           tier="P" if in_tab else "S")
        # prompt 占比：正文只举 cap1 的 69.9% 当例子，其余不逐条印
        ck("%s 复用率(prompt 占比)" % r["run"], "%.1f" % (float(r["reuse_frac"]) * 100),
           tier="P" if r["run"] == "cap1" else "S")
    hc = [r for r in load(os.path.join(ROOT, "paper/data/reuse.csv"))
          if r["family"] == "hicache"]
    bad = [r for r in hc if r["contested"] == "CONTESTED"]
    good = [r for r in hc if r["contested"] != "CONTESTED"]
    # host-tier 的【兑现率区间】：正文 §model 印的是 72.8--92.5%（全部 19 条 host-tier
    # run）与 90.2--92.4%（跨控制器区间的三个静态点）。按正文的口径（百分比、1 位小数）
    # 检查；reuse_frac（占 prompt 的比例）正文不印，降为次要档。
    # 2026-09-24 之前这里查的是 reuse_frac 的原始小数字符串，而 "0.5" 这种
    # 1 位小数写法在全篇到处都是 —— 那种检查等于永远通过，抓不到任何东西。
    for tag, sel in (("受损", bad), ("干净", good)):
        ck("%s host-tier 复用下界(占 prompt)" % tag, "%.1f" % (min(float(r["reuse_frac"]) for r in sel) * 100), tier="S")
        ck("%s host-tier 复用上界(占 prompt)" % tag, "%.1f" % (max(float(r["reuse_frac"]) for r in sel) * 100), tier="S")
    allhc = hc
    ck("host-tier 兑现下界", "%.1f" % (min(float(r["realized_frac"]) for r in allhc) * 100))
    ck("host-tier 兑现上界", "%.1f" % (max(float(r["realized_frac"]) for r in allhc) * 100))
    st3 = [r for r in hc if r["run"] in ("pairB_cap2hc", "cap3_hc", "cap4_hc")]
    ck("静态 host-tier 兑现下界", "%.1f" % (min(float(r["realized_frac"]) for r in st3) * 100))
    ck("静态 host-tier 兑现上界", "%.1f" % (max(float(r["realized_frac"]) for r in st3) * 100))

    # ---- reuse_x.csv：第二条 trace ----
    for r in load(os.path.join(ROOT, "paper/data/reuse_x.csv")):
        # 正文印的是「74.8% 掉到 64.0%」两个端点与逐段斜率，中间两条只在 slope.csv 里
        ck("xtrace %s 兑现率" % r["run"], "%.1f" % (float(r["realized_frac"]) * 100),
           tier="P" if r["run"] in ("realhr_cap2", "realhr_cap5") else "S")

    # ---- slope.csv ----
    for r in load(os.path.join(ROOT, "paper/data/slope.csv")):
        ck("斜率 %s %s->%s" % (r["trace"], r["r0"], r["r1"]),
           r["slope_pp_per_unit"].lstrip("-"))

    # ---- 表 I 的每一行：墙钟 / 并发 / SLO 三个显示列 ----
    # 2026-09-24 补：no-control 那一行的 conc 曾是 1.69，而 1.69 是
    # num_running_reqs 的均值 —— 也就是标题里写明「Conc. 不是它」的那个量。
    # 那种错（同一列两个口径）本脚本抓不到，但「表里的数与数据文件对不上」能抓到，
    # 所以把三列都纳入。wall/conc/slo 在表里印 1 位小数。
    runs_csv = {r["run"]: r for r in load(os.path.join(ROOT, "paper/data/runs.csv"))}
    # table1.csv 覆盖 runs.csv 的全部行；正文的表只印其中一部分。
    # pairA_cap2hc 被 pairB 在两个轴上都支配，正文【不印它】（只在候选锚点里出现），
    # 所以这两项是预期内的不匹配，跳过。
    NOT_PRINTED = {"pairA_cap2hc"}
    for r in load(os.path.join(ROOT, "paper/data/table1.csv")):
        k = r["run"]
        if k in NOT_PRINTED:
            continue
        ck("表 I %s 的墙钟" % k, "%.1f" % float(r["wall_min"]), "sec_results.tex")
        ck("表 I %s 的 SLO" % k, "%.1f" % float(r["slo_pct"]), "sec_results.tex")
        if k in runs_csv:
            ck("表 I %s 的并发" % k, "%.1f" % float(runs_csv[k]["conc"]), "sec_results.tex")

    # ---- table1.csv：表 I 的 Δ ----
    for r in load(os.path.join(ROOT, "paper/data/table1.csv")):
        if r.get("delta_pp"):
            ck("表 I %s 的 Δ" % r["run"], r["delta_pp"].lstrip("-"), "sec_results.tex")

    # ---- occupancy.csv 的臂数（正文两处引了「二十三条臂」）----
    occ = load(os.path.join(ROOT, "paper/data/occupancy.csv"))
    ck("occupancy.csv 的臂数", len(occ), "sec_results.tex")

    # ---- tier25.csv：表 II 的每一列（2026-09-24 补上生成器 gen_tier25.py 后纳入）----
    for r in load(os.path.join(ROOT, "paper/data/tier25.csv")):
        ck("表 II %s 墙钟" % r["run"], r["wall_min"], "sec_discussion.tex")
        ck("表 II %s 并发" % r["run"], r["conc"], "sec_discussion.tex")
        ck("表 II %s SLO" % r["run"], r["slo_pct"], "sec_discussion.tex")
        ck("表 II %s 段SD" % r["run"], r["seg_sd_pp"], "sec_discussion.tex")

    # ---- gate_attrib.csv 注释行里落定的两个中位数 ----
    # 「两个中位数会随集合变」（§V 的稳健性声明）用的就是这两行；它们只存在于
    # CSV 的注释里，所以单独解析。all_rows_median 这一行是 2026-09-24 才补上的 ——
    # 在此之前正文引用的「every run in that file」两个数在文件里根本查不到。
    ga = open(os.path.join(ROOT, "paper/data/gate_attrib.csv"), encoding="utf-8").read()
    for tag in ("eight_median", "all_rows_median"):
        mt = re.search(r"^# %s.*?valve_pct=([\d.]+),projection_over_pct=([\d.]+)" % tag,
                       ga, re.M)
        if mt:
            ck("gate_attrib %s 阀门" % tag, mt.group(1), "sec_results.tex")
            ck("gate_attrib %s 投影超预算" % tag, mt.group(2), "sec_results.tex")
        else:
            fails.append(("gate_attrib.csv 缺 %s 行" % tag, "-", "sec_results.tex"))

    # ---- gate_attrib.csv：foyer_hc 的饿死守卫三件套（§V 用它论证「超预算时只有它能进」）----
    gah = {r["run"]: r for r in load(os.path.join(ROOT, "paper/data/gate_attrib.csv"))}
    if "pois200_foyer_hc" in gah:
        r = gah["pois200_foyer_hc"]
        ck("foyer_hc 被强制准入数", r["forced_admits"], "sec_results.tex")
        ck("foyer_hc 总准入数", r["total_admits"], "sec_results.tex")
        ck("foyer_hc 投影超预算周期数", r["cycles_over_budget"], "sec_results.tex")
        ck("foyer_hc 超预算时放行数", r["admitted_while_over"], "sec_results.tex")
        ck("foyer_hc 被强制准入占比", "%.1f" % (100.0 * int(r["forced_admits"]) / int(r["total_admits"])),
           "sec_results.tex")

    # ---- hitrate.csv ----
    # 论文逐条印的是这十条（§related 的 80.8->32.8 与 host tier 的 80.7/79.6/78.7、
    # §V 的 80.6 与 bres 的 80.2）；其余是 2026-09-24 补进文件、正文未逐条印的臂。
    HIT_PRINTED = {"pois200_cap1", "pois200_cap2", "pois200_cap3_fixed", "pois200_cap4",
                   "pois200_cap5", "pois200_pairB_cap2hc", "pois200_cap3_hc",
                   "pois200_cap4_hc", "pois200_foyer_hc", "pois200_foyer_bres"}
    for r in load(os.path.join(ROOT, "paper/data/hitrate.csv")):
        ck("round-1+ %s" % r["run"], r["observed_round1_pct"],
           tier="P" if r["run"] in HIT_PRINTED else "S")

    # ---- toolwait.csv ----
    for r in load(os.path.join(ROOT, "paper/data/toolwait.csv")):
        for c in ("demand_med_s", "demand_mean_s", "measured_mean_s"):
            ck("toolwait %s %s" % (r["run"], c), r[c])
        # §background 的「中位 1.001 / 两个均值之比 1.065」
        if r["run"] == "pois200_foyer_hc":
            ck("toolwait foyer_hc 比值中位", "%.3f" % float(r["ratio_med"]), "sec_background.tex")
            ck("toolwait foyer_hc 均值之比", "%.3f" % float(r["ratio_ratio_of_means"]), "sec_background.tex")

    # ---- idle_pooled.csv ----
    for r in load(os.path.join(ROOT, "paper/data/idle_pooled.csv")):
        # 正文印 all_runs；minus_* 是「去掉被争用标记的 run 之后结论不变」的稳健性
        # 变体，只在 §repro 里被描述，没有逐条印数
        for c in ("n_segments", "mean_s", "median_s", "pct_idle_time_in_gt20s"):
            ck("idle %s %s" % (r["set"], c), r[c],
               tier="P" if r["set"] == "all_runs" else "S")

    # ---- decomp.csv：三个区间 ----
    d = load(os.path.join(ROOT, "paper/data/decomp.csv"))
    T13 = {"cap1", "cap2", "cap3_fixed", "cap4", "cap5", "pairB_cap2hc", "cap3_hc",
           "cap4_hc", "foyer_hc", "foyer_hz1", "foyer_hz2", "foyer_hz0", "foyer_bres"}
    d13 = [r for r in d if r["run"] in T13]
    for desc, vals in (
            ("τ₁ 上界", [float(r["step_back_ms"]) for r in d13 if r["step_back_ms"]]),
            ("τ₁ 下界", [float(r["step_back_ms"]) for r in d13 if r["step_back_ms"]]),
            ("τ₂ 上界", [float(r["step_perreq_ms"]) for r in d13 if r["step_perreq_ms"]]),
            ("τ₂ 下界", [float(r["step_perreq_ms"]) for r in d13 if r["step_perreq_ms"]])):
        ck(desc, max(vals) if "上界" in desc else min(vals))
    ex = [float(r["excess_pct"]) for r in d13 if r["excess_pct"]]
    ck("τ₁/τ₂ 超出下界", min(ex)); ck("τ₁/τ₂ 超出上界", max(ex))

    # ---- concurrency.csv 的计数 ----
    c = load(os.path.join(ROOT, "paper/data/concurrency.csv"))
    ck("CONTESTED 总数", sum(1 for r in c if r["verdict"] == "CONTESTED"),
       "sec_discussion.tex")

    # ---- projection.csv ----
    for r in load(os.path.join(ROOT, "paper/data/projection.csv")):
        if r["run"] == "pois200_foyer_hc":
            ck("foyer_hc 投影倍数", r["ratio"])
            ck("foyer_hc free%", r["free_pct"])
            # 正文 §V：「the growth reserve, which alone is 26,762 tokens, 28% of the budget」
            ck("foyer_hc 增长预留 tokens", r.get("growth_p50", ""))

    # ---- 图是否比数据旧 ----
    # 2026-09-24 碰巧发现：数据在 19:05/19:22 改过而图停在 18:43，正文的数与图对不上，
    # 而没有任何东西会提醒。图的输入是 decomp.csv / runs.csv / occupancy.csv 等。
    figs = glob.glob(os.path.join(ROOT, "paper", "figs", "fig*.pdf"))
    if figs:
        newest_data = max((os.path.getmtime(f) for f in glob.glob(
            os.path.join(ROOT, "paper", "data", "*.csv"))), default=0)
        stale = [os.path.basename(f) for f in figs if os.path.getmtime(f) < newest_data]
        checks += 1
        if stale:
            import datetime as _dt
            fails.append(("图比 paper/data 旧（需重跑 make_figs.py）",
                          "%d 张" % len(stale),
                          _dt.datetime.fromtimestamp(newest_data).strftime("%H:%M")))
        elif VERBOSE:
            print("  OK   图不比数据旧")

    print("共 %d 项检查：%d 项 MISS（正文引用过、却没找到，必须处置），"
          "%d 项次要未印（论文只印聚合值，属预期）。" % (checks, len(fails), len(secondary)))
    for desc, val, where in fails:
        print("  MISS %-56s %-12s (%s)" % (desc, val, where))
    if secondary and (VERBOSE or fails):
        print("  —— 次要（不计入退出码，供扫一眼）——")
        for desc, val, where in secondary:
            print("  ..   %-56s %-12s (%s)" % (desc, val, where))
    return 1 if fails else 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

# 主张 → 证据 审计（2026-09-23）

> 目的：写正文之前，把活下来的每一条主张拉出来逐条查。
> 判据是四样，**缺任何一样就降级或删掉**：
>
> 1. **实测数字**（不是推论）
> 2. **对照**（不是孤立观测）
> 3. **反例检查**（我们自己试过推翻它没有）
> 4. **可复现路径**（命令 / 脚本 / run 名）
>
> 这个项目的**六条**撤回（步成本的分母、`tool_wait` 0.1s→16.85s、oracle 单调性/增长敏感度、
> 重复计费、空转率单调性、以及「驱逐→重算」那次错误的反驳）都是因为缺了第 3 条。
> 六条的清单与论文 §讨论 逐条对应；此前这里写「五条」，漏了「步成本的分母」那条
> —— 而它恰好是唯一改过头条数字的一次（2026-09-24 对齐）。

---

## C1. 墙钟可以拆成「有效工作 + 空转」两部分

**数字**（2026-09-23 修正为采样跨度口径）：`cap4_hc 202.8 + 16.1 = 218.9`（采样跨度，墙钟 213.0）；`foyer_hc 250.1 + 47.4 = 297.5`（墙钟 291.3）；`cap1 340.0 + 193.6 = 533.6`（墙钟 527.4）。各 run 的跨度比墙钟长约 6 分钟，是采样器早于第一个请求启动造成的。
**对照**：11 条静态 + 9 条 Foyer，全表一致
**反例检查**：`bres` 是反例形态的反例——它空转压到 10.1% 但有效工作涨到 271 分钟，**证明两项独立**
**路径**：`scripts/frontier.py`；空转从 `metrics.csv` 的 `sglang:num_running_reqs == 0` 段累加

**状态：可用。** 注意措辞——这是**分解**，不是"模型"。

---

## C2. 步时间可以被两个独立方法测到，且两者排序一致

**数字**（反推 / 逐请求）：

```
cap4_hc   29.0 / 25.7 ms        cap5   35.9 / 31.2 ms   ← 两个方法都判异常
cap3_hc   27.3 / 24.5 ms        bres   34.2 / 31.3 ms   ← 都判第二异常
cap2      26.9 / 22.8 ms        hz1    28.5 / 24.6 ms
foyer_hc  28.2 / 23.8 ms
```

**方法一** = busy 时间 × **busy 期间条件**在跑均值 ÷ 产出 token（从 `metrics.csv` + `steps.jsonl`）。⚠ 2026-09-23 修正：原来用的是全时段均值，等于把空转采样也算进去，τ 系统性偏低一个 busy 占比（cap1 从 28.8 变 18.5）
**方法二** = 逐请求 `(total_duration_ms − first_token_ms) / (output_len_actual − 1)`

**系统性差 8–16%，排序完全一致。**

\subsection{Route 1: the accounting base}

Our controller's projection (Eq.~\ref{eq:proj}) charges a session for its full
context even while it is parked in a tool wait, when its KV is evictable and
therefore absent from the engine's reported occupancy. We argued this was a
double charge and removed it, admitting on engine telemetry alone.

The charge is not double. Removing it did admit more---median engine occupancy
rose from 49.2\% to 56.9\% of the pool---and the round-1+ prefix hit rate held at
80.2\% against the 80.6\% of the run that keeps the charge, so no eviction spiral occurred. But the run was
4.2 minutes \emph{slower} and lost 6.0\,pp of SLO. Per \S\ref{sec:model}, the
extra occupancy raised both $\overline{R}$ (1.35 to 1.48) and $\tau_2$
(23.8\,ms to 31.3\,ms), and the two cancelled.

That last sentence is the one place where this route's conclusion depends on a
contaminated measurement, and we mark it. The arm that tests this route
(\texttt{bres}) is one of the four flagged $^\ddagger$ in
Table~\ref{tab:runs}: its 31.3\,ms $\tau_2$ was measured under host
co-tenancy, and the same arm measured clean would plausibly sit near 25\,ms
\paragraph{Application-level dependencies and replay.} Serving an LLM
\emph{application} rather than a request means the scheduler sees dependencies
between calls \cite{lin2024parrot}, and agentic traces have structure that
request-level benchmarks do not capture \cite{wang2026xperf}. Our replay
preserves that structure but fixes it in advance, which is exactly the
limitation these works would press on.

\paragraph{Prefix caching.} The prefix cache is often proposed as the signal
an admission policy should use. What we measure is that the \emph{realized}
reuse is a function of the admission policy and of the host tier, while the
\emph{planned} reuse is a constant of the trace; an earlier draft of this
paper conflated the two and got the direction wrong in both. The trace plans
a round-1-and-later hit rate of 80.9\%. Without HiCache the realized
round-1+ rate follows the plan only at the loosest cap and then collapses:
80.8\%, 78.5\%, 70.6\%, 56.0\%, 32.8\% for caps of one through five. With
HiCache it tracks the plan at every cap we ran: 80.7\%, 79.6\%, 78.7\% for
caps of two, three and four. Our controller sits at 80.6\%.
So the honest statement is not that admission does not move this quantity---%
\texttt{cap4} alone moves it 25 points---but that the host tier is what keeps
it from moving, which is the same mechanism \S\ref{sec:model} and
\S\ref{sec:criterion} keep running into. A configuration without a host tier
that admits four sessions does not have the cache that a host-tier one at the
same concurrency does. All eight numbers in this paragraph are the
\emph{observed} per-step hit rate (\texttt{server\_prefix\_hit\_rate} in the
per-step logs), unweighted mean over rounds $\ge 1$; the planned rate is the
trace constant 80.9\% and does not move with the cap. \S\ref{sec:model} uses
a different convention---all steps, token-weighted, so that reuse and
recomputation partition the prompt---under which the same collapse reads
92.5\%$\to$36.4\% of the trace's bound for the device-only caps and
92.4\%$\to$90.2\% for the static host-tier caps. We state both because the paper's earlier
drafts mixed them. We note the boundary of this claim---we varied admission policy,
not eviction policy, and SGLang exposes alternate eviction policies we did not
test. A characterization of production KV-cache traffic at a large cloud
provider finds that eviction-policy design is ``highly workload-dependent''
and that reuse patterns differ sharply by request category
\cite{wang2025kvcachewild}; that is reason to expect a single trace family to
\section{Decomposing the Wall Clock}
\label{sec:model}

\subsection{Two components}

We split the wall clock at every sampled instant according to whether the
engine has any request in flight:
\begin{equation}
  W \;=\; W_{\text{busy}} \;+\; W_{\text{idle}} .
  \label{eq:split}
\end{equation}
Fig.~\ref{fig:decomp} shows the split for every completed run. Two things are
immediately visible and both matter later.

First, idle time is not noise. Across the runs it spans 7.4\% to 41.9\% of
the sampled span---7.4\% for the fastest configuration, 36.3\% for the
slowest, and 41.9\% for the one that idles most (all-throttles-off). The
denominator here
is the span the sampler observed, which runs about six minutes longer than the
request wall clock of Table~\ref{tab:runs}, so the fractions against that
wall clock are 7.6\% and 36.7\%---and it is not spread evenly.
Over 1{,}849 stretches the mean is 20.6\,s and 17.6\% of them exceed
20\,s, accounting for 69.6\% of all idle \emph{time}
(Fig.~\ref{fig:idle}). The tail, not the middle, is what a scheduler feels,
and it sits at the scale of the 15.8--16.9\,s mean tool wait (demanded and
measured; \texttt{toolwait.csv}). We report the mean and the share rather
than the median here because the median is not robust to which runs enter the
pool: it is 10.0\,s without \texttt{cap1} and 5.0--5.6\,s with it, since
that one run contributes 411 of the 1{,}849 stretches and is itself the
longest. The mean (18.3--21.0\,s) and the over-20\,s share of idle time
(64.8--70.6\%) move by little across the four poolings we report in
\texttt{idle\_pooled.csv}, so the claim that the tail dominates does not
depend on the choice. The 90th percentile moves with the median
(25.1--30.1\,s) and we give it as a range for the same reason.

Second, the two components move independently. \texttt{bres}
(\S\ref{sec:results}) reduces idle from 15.9\% to 10.1\%, recovering about 17
minutes---and is nonetheless \emph{slower} than its own baseline, because its
busy time grows by 21 minutes. Any account that treats ``more concurrency'' as
one thing will get this wrong. We flag one caveat on the numbers in this
paragraph: \texttt{bres} is one of the runs measured under host co-tenancy
(\S\ref{sec:repro}), and co-tenancy inflates $\tau$, which lengthens busy
time---so the 21-minute growth is an upper bound on the real one. The
qualitative point, that the two components move in opposite directions, is not
affected: idle falls by construction under a policy that keeps more sessions
live, and the run is slower overall, which is where the comparison starts. The
re-run (\S\ref{sec:repro}) will give the corrected split.

\begin{figure}[tb]
  \centering
  \includegraphics[width=\columnwidth]{figs/fig2_decomp.pdf}
  \caption{Wall clock split into busy and idle for every completed run,
  ordered by total duration. Idle is 7.4\% for the fastest configuration and
  36.3\% for the slowest. Note \texttt{bres}: it cuts idle to 10.1\% and is
  still slower than its own baseline, because busy time grows instead---and
  note that \texttt{bres} is one of the six hatched bars, whose busy time is
  itself inflated by host co-tenancy (\S\ref{sec:repro}); the hatch is the
  same $^\ddagger$ flag as in Table~\ref{tab:runs}.}
  \label{fig:decomp}
\end{figure}

\begin{figure}[tb]
  \centering
  \includegraphics[width=\columnwidth]{figs/fig4_idle_segments.pdf}
  \caption{Survival function of idle-stretch duration, pooled over all
  completed runs (1{,}849 stretches). Most stretches are short---the median is
  5--10\,s depending on which runs enter the pool, which is why we do not lead
  with it---but the minutes are not: 69.6\% of all idle \emph{time} sits in
  stretches longer than 20\,s (64.8--70.6\% across the four poolings in
  \texttt{idle\_pooled.csv}), and the mean stretch is 20.6\,s, the same order
  as the 15.8--16.9\,s mean tool wait. The cliff is the 5\,s sampling floor.}
  \label{fig:idle}
\end{figure}

\subsection{What determines busy time}
